- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §4/§8.1 (P1.8b) · **squat, jump_squat, chair_squat all ship `rep.metric: "knee_L"` (+`knee_R` fallback)** — the legacy rep_counter joint (`left_knee`, right fallback; rep_counter.py:15,162-164). RESOLVES the FOOTNOTE 2 / rep-metric SPEC GAP: measured `knee_avg` vs `knee_L` on all 9 goldens — `knee_avg` matches on the 3 squat clips but UNDERCOUNTS chair (Chair_Squat_goodform_sideview1 2→1) and jump (jump_squat_goodform_sideview2 2→0), because with `knee_avg` the bilateral-gate "other knee" flips (session.ts:207) and the mean absorbs the deeper leg. `knee_L` is the faithful port and keeps all 9 green. `squat.json` v6 supersedes `squat.v5.json`'s illustrative `knee_avg`; v5 stays as the P1.7 worked-example fixture. **squat.json also DROPS the worked example's `minRepMs: 900` and `maxRepMs: 12000` — neither is ported** (legacy `_min_rep_interval`=0.45s→450 only; rep_counter.py:148, and the inventory flags 900 as squat.v5-only and maxRepMs as having no legacy source, lines 39/176-178). `minRepMs:900` is behaviourally live — `max(450, minRepMs)` (fsm.ts:96) would double the rep-interval floor with no port basis, so it's removed; the ported 450 engine floor stands. (T3 P1.8b finding V1/V2.)
- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §7.6 (P1.8b) · traces.replay drives all 9 parity traces through COMPILED §4 definitions (test/definitions/{squat,jump_squat,chair_squat}.json), lint-gated before replay; hand-built `parity-configs.ts` retired; `scripts/fsm-parity-debug.ts` repointed to compiled defs. reps-exact + scoreRange green 9/9.
- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §3.8 (P1.8b) · **Only squat is scored faithfully today.** jump depth/trunk/valgus are per-frame gated on `is_loading`/`currently_airborne`/`just_landed` (form_analyzer.py:441-504) and chair depth is target-relative (`full_at=target+10`, form_analyzer.py:123-159) — neither is expressible in the scoring model (fixed-number curve x, per-rep aggregates, no airborne state). jump & chair ship exact COUNTING + expressible faults (jump-local valgus −0.15/−0.25; chair valgus −0.15/−0.30) + a **documented INTERIM/placeholder depth scorer**, gated on reps+scoreRange only. The interim curve *numbers* ARE §8.1-traceable — jump `[[90,100],[140,40]]` from §D (form_analyzer.py:450 anchor 90/span 60, :445 loading-fault 40, LOADING_DEPTH_MIN 140); chair `[[110,100],[125,40],[155,0]]` from §C's fallback-target path (CHAIR_FALLBACK_TARGET 100 +TOLERANCE 10, +SHALLOW_MARGIN 25, STANDING 155) — but they are INTERIM because the per-frame airborne gating (jump) and calibrated-target anchoring (chair) that Python applies around them cannot be reproduced, so they must NOT be read as faithful per-rep scorers. Chair trunk-lean and jump trunk-lean are `(trunk − shin)` relative comparisons the DSL cannot express (no binary subtraction) → deferred with the same template work. See the two SPEC GAPs below. (T3 P1.8b finding V3.)

- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §7.4/§7.5/§10 (P1.8b Done gate) · **Kd chose OPTION B** (per the P1.8b kickoff, which relaxes the spec gate for this task). P1.8b lands with the CURRENT 9 clips green — **reps-exact + view + scoreRange enforced**. Deferred as tracked follow-ups to the P4 exercise line: fault-multiset exact (§7.4, needs the jump-airborne / chair-target template work), per-rep score within ±3 (§7.5, plus its undefined per-frame→per-rep extraction rule), and the full ≥6 squat + 4 jump + 4 chair session count (§7.5, have 3+3+3). **G3 signed off** (reduced assertion surface accepted); the ±3 extraction-rule decision is not needed until P4. The strict §7.4/§7.5 gate remains the P4 target; the shortfall warns loudly in traces.replay and is logged above.

- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §7.6/§1.3 I5 (P1.9) · **Perf gate armed.** test/perf.test.ts replays a real golden looped to 9000 frames (10 min @15fps) through the real squat engine. Measured on the dev box: **p50 ≈ 0.011 ms, p95 ≈ 0.03 ms** — ~100× under budget. INTERPRETATION (SPEC GAP resolution): §7.6's "≤3 ms scaled to the CI baseline machine" is enforced here as a **regression TRIPWIRE** (`p95 ≤ 3 ms` on CI); the true ≤3 ms *mid-Android (₹12k)* device budget (I5) is verified at the **P5 §3.2 mobile spike** on real hardware (a dev/CI box is faster than mid-Android, so CI-pass is necessary, not sufficient). Consistent with the playbook's "a model-estimated frame rate is not a result" stance.
- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §7.6/I5 (P1.9) · **Heap-flat gate.** Asserts heap delta over the 9000-frame run. `vitest.config.ts` runs the suite in forks with `--expose-gc`, so the **STRICT bound (<5 MB, forced GC) is the one CI actually enforces** — reproducibly **≈ −25.8 MB** (GC reclaims all per-frame garbage). The loose **<40 MB** path is only a fallback for a runner without `--expose-gc`; its natural-GC delta is **NOISY (observed roughly −5 to +25 MB across back-to-back runs)** and must NOT be read as a stable figure — it exists to avoid flaking, not to catch leaks. (T3 P1.9: the loose path alone was leak-permeable; strict is now armed.) **The R5.5 ring-buffer refactor was NOT needed** — per-frame allocations are fully reclaimed under forced GC; that deferral is closed as "measured, no action required at P1.9," kept honest by the strict gate going forward.
- 2026-07-09 · Part 2 §7.6/§3.6 (P1.9) · **Fuzz pass green.** test/fuzz.test.ts drives seeded (mulberry32) NaN / dropout-burst / 8–40 fps jitter on the squat/chair/jump good goldens (40 seeds × 4 variants each) + a recovery case. All three invariants hold: no throw (I6), rep count never exceeds the clean baseline, counting recovers after a full dropout. **Confirms the 2026-07-08 debounce decision**: raw frame-count debounce (3 down/2 up) does NOT let fps jitter increase counts — no time-normalization change needed.

- 2026-07-09 · CLAUDE.md P0.2 / migration stance (P1.10a) · **The salvage `web` app is held OUT of the workspace lint gate** (root script `turbo run lint --filter=!web`) after `frontend/` → `apps/web` (v1 §4/§13 "migrated in place"). The old SPA carries 90 pre-existing eslint errors (react-hooks/set-state-in-effect, no-unused-vars); mass-fixing them is forbidden by R1.1 and the "don't polish code being rebuilt" stance, and a permanently-red CI destroys the gate's signal. `web` keeps a locally-runnable `lint` script; it is not part of typecheck/test (no such scripts). **Re-entry trigger:** remove the `--filter=!web` exclusion once `web` is migrated onto the strict `packages/config` eslint/tsconfig presets during the P1.10b→P2 web rebuild. Strangler-fig, not a lowered bar.

- 2026-07-10 · Part 2 §3.1/§3.2 + Appendix A (P1.10 PROVE fix) · **Legs-occluded honesty gap fixed at the client bridge layer.** Kd's live run showed "✓ Good Form 100%" while sitting at a desk (legs invisible): the engine correctly nulls the metric and refuses to count (§3.2 gate — proven by replaying the live recording: 0 reps), but ingest-level visibilityOk stays true (frames are valid), no fault fires, and the UI read that silence as "Good Form". The legacy form_analyzer's explicit leg-visibility warning had been narrowed in the port to ingest-invalid frames only. FIX: SessionController tracks a metric-unusable streak (rep metric + compiled fallback absent from FrameResult.signals); after 3 consecutive frames (§3.1's own count) it shows Appendix A's "cannot see your legs clearly — step back" and withdraws the form verdict (form_correct null). Implemented in the WEB BRIDGE, not the engine — engine behavior (no decision) was already correct; only the presentation lied. Revisit moving the cue into §3.2 itself when mobile needs it (P5).
- 2026-07-10 · Part 2 §7.3/§7.5 (P1.10 PROVE) · **Regression-trace class added.** test/traces/regression/ holds live on-device recordings with NO Python sidecar (the legacy server no longer exists to produce one); traces.replay runs full §7.4 assertions on them but excludes them from §7.5 parity certification counts and the sidecar stage-parity checks. First fixture: squat_sitting_idle_desk_nocount.jsonl (600 frames, expected reps 0) — the §7.3 partial-visibility case the fixture matrix was missing. Kd's live squat recordings confirmed counting is threshold-correct (only sub-100° reps count, per the ported legacy constants) — shallow-rep UX feedback deferred to the P4 §9.1 tuning protocol, NOT hand-edited (R5.4).

- 2026-07-10 · (tooling; P1.10c gate) · **Workspace zod stays at v3; apps/web's `zod@^4.4.1` dependency REMOVED.** Grep proved zod is imported nowhere in apps/web/src — the dep was vestigial salvage. Web now consumes `workoutSyncPayloadSchema` via `@app/shared` (workspace:*), running on shared's zod@3; shared/api/engine untouched. A zod-4 migration, if ever wanted, is its own workspace-wide card. Approved at P1.10c plan gate (option A).
- 2026-07-10 · Part 4 §3.4 / v1 §5.3 (P1.10c) · **`defsVersion` = constant 1 for the static-bridge era.** The sync payload requires a positive-int bundle version, but P1.10b ships definitions as compiled-in package exports — no bundle_version exists until the P2.2 catalog bundle API (Part 2 §9.3). `STATIC_DEFS_BUNDLE_VERSION = 1` in syncClient.js; replaced when real bundles land. Approved at P1.10c plan gate.
- 2026-07-10 · R10.3 (P1.10c) · **Sync-queue retry policy.** Transient failures (network, 5xx, 401/408/429) retain the entry and HALT the flush (order preserved — later workouts never jump ahead). Any other 4xx is a permanent rejection of that exact payload: the entry moves to a parked localStorage list — never dropped (R10.3), never a poison pill blocking the queue. Payloads failing the shared contract schema locally are parked the same way. Approved at P1.10c plan gate.
- 2026-07-10 · v1 §5.3 / Part 6 §3.6 (P1.10c) · **All-log-only workouts are NOT synced** (no engine SetSummaries → nothing engine-verified to send; `sets: []` would record an empty engine workout). The legacy completeSession call still records them until P2.8. Approved at P1.10c plan gate.

- 2026-07-10 · v1 §6.1 / R3.3 (P1.10d) · **Sync auth seam:** `SYNC_DEV_USER_ID` env var — when set (dev/test only; config boot REFUSES it in production) every `/v1/workouts/sync` request authenticates as that user; unset, the route answers 401 and stays dark until P2.1 wires real cookie authn. P2.1 replaces exactly the one seam block in routes.ts with the real authenticate preHandler and must re-prove the cross-tenant denial test end-to-end. Approved at P1.10d plan gate. (Corrects the record: earlier prompts cited a "2A" DECISIONS entry that was never actually written.)
- 2026-07-10 · Part 4 §3.5 / R4.5 (P1.10d) · **R4.5's "workout-sync upsert per Part 4 §4 canonical SQL" is a dangling pointer** — §4's four canonical operations don't include sync. Implemented from §3.5's own contract text: workout `ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`, sets keyed `(workout_id, set_index)`. §3.5's DDL omits the unique constraint that keying requires → migration `0002_workout_sets_uq` adds `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX workout_sets_workout_set_uq ON workout_sets (workout_id, set_index)`.
- 2026-07-10 · Part 4 §3.4/§8, Part 2 §6, 2B App A (P1.10d) · **Minimal exercises seed: only the 3 engine exercises** (squat F1/T1/MET 6.0✱, jump_squat F1/T1/8.0, chair_squat F1/T1/5.0✱ — family/tier from Part 2 §6 Tier-1 table, METs from 2B Appendix A verbatim). Full 58-row catalog seed stays with its owning task. `name_key` = `exercise.<slug>` (extends the `plan.<code>` convention). Approved at P1.10d plan gate.
- 2026-07-10 · v1 §5.3 / R10.3 interplay (P1.10d) · **Unknown exercise slug does NOT 4xx the sync** — the client parks permanently-rejected payloads, so a missing catalog row would strand real workouts. The workout is accepted; unknown-slug sets are skipped, `quality_flags += 'unknown_exercise'`, workout aggregates cover persisted sets only. Approved at P1.10d plan gate.
- 2026-07-10 · Part 4 §3.5 / 2B §2.2 (P1.10d) · **`kcal_point`/`kcal_calc_version` = null at sync** until the 2B calories port (P2.6) — needs body weight + MET method not yet in the new API. Columns are nullable by design. Aggregates (`sets_count`, `total_reps`, `avg_form_score`, `duration_ms`) are derived server-side from the persisted sets (R3.1); `ended_at` stays null (spec names no source for it in the payload). Approved at P1.10d plan gate.

- 2026-07-10 · Part 2 §2.4 / Part 4 §3.5 (P1.10d T3) · **Sync contract tightened with DDL-derived bounds:** setIndex/reps ≤ 32767 (smallint), durationMs/holdMs/tempoMsAvg/definitionVersion/defsVersion ≤ 2147483647 (int4), sets[] non-empty (the P1.10c all-log-only decision, now enforced server-side too), sets[] ≤ 32767 (sets_count smallint), duplicate setIndex rejected (it would silently drop rows under the §3.5 ON CONFLICT upsert and desync the server-derived aggregates). Rationale: a value Zod passed but PG overflowed became a 500, which the client's R10.3 retry policy treats as transient — a poison payload would halt the queue forever. Every bound cites the §3.5 column type; nothing invented.
- 2026-07-10 · R3.3 (P1.10d T3) · **Seam gate inverted to explicit-allow:** SYNC_DEV_USER_ID now requires NODE_ENV to be EXPLICITLY development or test (checked against the raw env, not the parsed default) — a production box that merely forgot NODE_ENV can no longer boot with the seam honored. Tested (production AND omitted cases).

## Pending (SPEC GAPs raised, awaiting Kd)

- **SPEC GAP (P1.8b→P4): jump-squat airborne-state scoring/faults.** Stiff-landing, loading-depth window, just-landed landing score, and `(trunk−shin)` relative lean all need per-frame airborne gating (hip_elevation crossing 0.15, landing window `0<elev<0.30`) that the current scoring/fault template cannot express. The P1.8b jump definition ships counting + an interim depth curve only; the airborne apparatus is deferred to the P4 jump task with a template/DSL extension. (R5.6: template gap, NOT an engine special-case.)
- **SPEC GAP (P1.8b→P4): chair adaptive depth scoring.** The legacy chair depth curve is anchored to the C2 `target` (full credit at `target+10`, shallow at `target+25`); the scoring schema's curve x-values are fixed numbers (only the fault DSL has `target+N`). Chair depth ships an interim fixed-fallback-target curve; target-relative score curves are deferred to the P4 template work. (Independent of the C2-formula SPEC GAP below — even the §3.5 formula's target can't be threaded into a score curve today.)
- **SPEC GAP (P1.8b gate debt (a) RECLASSIFIED — needs a decision):** the "±3-vs-Python per-rep score" assertion has **no defined extraction rule**. Python emits a per-FRAME `form_score`; the engine emits one per-REP score from cycle aggregates — there is no spec rule for deriving a Python per-rep score from the per-frame series, and the two methodologies diverge (Python neutral-80 at the counting frame near lockout vs engine's bottom-aggregate score). §7.4 declares only `scoreRange`; §7.5 doesn't specify the extraction. **NOT added in P1.8b** — the scoreRange gate remains the score assertion. RECOMMEND ratifying: "Python rep score = min `form_score` within that rep's descent→completion window," then assert `|engine − python| ≤ 3` for squat only (jump/chair scoring is interim, above). Awaiting Kd.

- **§7.5 trace-count shortfall (P1.8b Option-B debt, T3 finding G1) — hard gate NOT met:** §7.5 / Part 0 #4 / the P1.8b card require ≥**6 squat + 4 jump + 4 chair** recorded parity sessions; the repo has **3 + 3 + 3**. "9/9 green" = all *present* clips pass, NOT certification. Per the Option-B scope Kd set, this is tracked debt, not a blocker — traces.replay now warns the per-exercise shortfall loudly (no longer the tautology `files.length ≥ 0`, T3 finding G2). Certification unblocks when Kd records the remaining ~3 squat + 1 jump + 1 chair clips (front views included).
- **G3 sign-off needed (T3 finding):** P1's "all invariants CI-enforced" is only PARTIALLY true for these three exercises — reps-exact + view + scoreRange are enforced; fault multisets (I2 exact), ±3 score, and phase-timing tolerances are deferred (below). Needs Kd's explicit acceptance that P1.8b lands with this reduced assertion surface rather than an implicit pass.
- **P1.8b gate debt, still open (T3 P1.6a):** (b) Fault multisets have zero trace coverage — blocked on the same jump-airborne / chair-target template work as the fault mappings above (faultsPending stays). (c) Phase timings are asserted only structurally; a tolerance must be declared before any trace asserts them (§7.4 names none). (d) Kd should spot-check 2–3 re-recorded goldens' expected.reps against the raw videos (gate and fixtures changed in the same PR).

- **SPEC GAP (P1.5, needs ratification at PR #9 merge):** §3.5 says `torso_height = |shoulder_y − hip_y|` but §3.4 row 16 says elevation is normalized "exactly as captured by _StandingCalibration", and the Python capture is `|avg_ankle_y − avg_shoulder_y|` (pose_ws.py:204-207) — the two spec sentences conflict. IMPLEMENTED: the Python capture (shoulder→ankle), because §7.5 parity compares against Python-derived elevations and would otherwise fail by a ~2–2.5× ratio. Merging PR #9 ratifies this; reopen if you read it differently.
- **SPEC GAP (P1.5, decision needed by P1.8b) — RESOLVED (b) at P1.8b:** C2 formula — spec §3.5 mandates mean-of-2-extremes clamped [80,120] (implemented, R0), but legacy chair calibration uses median + 5° buffer, no clamp. Chose **(b): assert chair reps-exact + scoreRange, defer any strict chair score parity.** Option (a) is not buildable in P1.8b — the engine's AdaptiveTarget implements only the §3.5 mean-of-2-clamp formula and the definition schema exposes no "median+5°" mode, so a "definition override" doesn't exist without engine+schema work; and the strict ±3 comparison is itself deferred (reclassified SPEC GAP above). The chair definition keeps the §3.5 `adaptive_target` (clamp[80,120], fallback 100) armed for when target-relative scoring lands. Revisit when the chair target-relative scoring gap is worked.

- Part 5 §1.2 vs Part 4 §3.3: plan-row codes for org USD book and org annual (×10) book — `plans` holds one currency+interval per row; codes like `org_micro_us_m` / `org_micro_in_y` need ratifying before P3.1.
- Part 4 §3.3: org plans' subscriber `entitlements` ("console features") shape unspecified — seeded `{}`.
- Part 4 §3.3: `name_key` convention unspecified — using `plan.<code>` until ratified.

Regression test: concurrent duplicate refresh.

This is NOT bit-identical to the old Chroma path (default L2 distance over NON-normalized MiniLM vectors) — rankings can differ in principle. Cosine-on-normalized is the conventional/recommended metric for MiniLM sentence embeddings and is what the ported retrieval quality was spot-checked against at ingest (squat→form_guides, protein→nutrition, sore→recovery top-1). Corrects the earlier, inaccurate claim that L2-normalize "matches sentence-transformers' encode() defaults" (encode defaults to normalize_embeddings=False).

TTL 24h. Cache hits consume a quota slot (increment-before-run doctrine) but skip the provider call and write NO cost event.

- 2026-07-12 · v1 §9.3 / Part 0 rule 4 (P2.5b) · Groq llama-3.1-8b-instant public list price used for cost_micro: $0.05/1M input + $0.08/1M output tokens (groq.com/pricing, 2026-07) as integer micro-USD-per-1M constants (50_000 / 80_000). - 2026-07-12 · v1 §6.1 (P2.5b T3 finding 2 — CORRECTS GAP-3) · The global answer cache's premise ("prompt carries no per-user variables") was FALSE — the prompt embedded displayName + weightKg, so a cached answer could leak one user's name/weight-derived numbers to another. FIX: displayName removed from the prompt entirely (advice-irrelevant, pure leak vector); units + weightKg stay in the prompt AND are folded into the cache key as a profile fingerprint. Residual "Groq spent + total DB failure" is inherent to a non-transactional external call; an OUTBOX is the eventual answer, owed with the billing/worker phase. cost_micro is now pure BigInt (round-half-up), no float touches money.

Acceptable in v1 (global limiter + quota bound it; not payment-adjacent so R3.5 doesn't mandate a key; web client not wired until P2.8). Add a client-generated idempotency key + short-window cap when the client is wired (P2.8) or the mobile card (P5).

## P2.6a — Nutrition and body rulings (2026-07-12)

- Part 2B §3.1/§3.4 vs v1 §6.1 · Meal photos are never persisted anywhere: no R2 object, DB value, cache value, log field, error body, or Sentry payload. The later and more-specific Trust Layer rule wins; photos travel as strict base64 request data, are decoded/analyzed in memory, then discarded.
- Part 2B §3.2 Stage 2 · Portion resolver ships reliable counts plus rung 1 user dishware, rung 3 Appendix B regional priors, and rung 4 global defaults. `portion_source` records the rung. Rung 2 anchor scaling is deferred because Stage 1 returns prose anchors without pixel geometry; follow-up requires a richer vision contract.
- Part 2B §3.2 Stage 3 · Nutrition arithmetic uses the exactly inventoried 130-row curated table first and cache-fronted OpenFoodFacts second. The misnamed salvage `usda.py` is OpenFoodFacts, not USDA. IFCT 2017 is a data-sourcing follow-up.
- v1 §9.3 · OpenFoodFacts calls are exempt from `api_cost_events`: the public API is zero-cost and cache-fronted. This is the explicit exception to the general external-call ledger rule; malformed/unavailable replies degrade to curated-only/empty results.
- Part 2B §3.2 Stage 5 · Per-user dishware memory and correction storage ship now. Confirmation-time changes and later PATCH changes preserve originals in `meal_log_corrections`; per-user bias adaptation and the global regression evaluation set are deferred until telemetry volume exists.
- Part 2B §3.5 · Failed parse or `photo_quality='poor'` issues a cryptographically random retake token stored only as a SHA-256-derived, user-bound Redis key. It is atomically single-use, expires after 10 minutes, bypasses exactly one `meal_scan` quota increment, cannot mint a second token, and fails closed when Redis cannot issue/consume it.
- Part 4 §3.6 / R4.2 · Migration 0005 adds `meal_logs.origin text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual' CHECK (origin IN ('photo','manual'))`. Photo-vs-manual is SQL-filtered by a real column, never a JSONB marker. Gamification derives `total_meals=count(*)` and `photo_meals_logged=count(*) FILTER (WHERE origin='photo')`.
- Part 4 §3.6 · The nutrition module owns `body_measurements` CRUD and keeps `users.weight_kg` synchronized to the latest non-null measurement. The users module remains profile-only; direct profile weight edits do not append measurement history.
- v1 §9.3 / P2.5b T3 precedent · A vision scan has no companion application row at spend time, so its `api_cost_events` record is inserted standalone immediately after the provider response and before any result is returned. This keeps the no-spend-escapes-the-ledger invariant; meal confirmation is a later, no-spend transaction.
- Part 2B §3.2 / Groq public pricing · Vision default remains `meta-llama/llama-4-scout-17b-16e-instruct`, with `MEAL_VISION_MODEL` as the swap lever. Groq lists Scout at $0.11/1M input and $0.34/1M output, encoded as integer 110,000/340,000 micro-USD-per-1M constants with BigInt round-half-up. Groq also announces deprecation on 2026-07-17; this is an operational swap risk, not permission to silently change the ruled default.
- 2026-07-12 · P2.2 GAP-2 SUPERSEDED (P2.6a) · Weight history now has ONE write path: body_measurements CRUD (nutrition module) keeps users.weight_kg synced to the latest non-null measurement; profile PATCH weightKg no longer appends a measurement row (it remains for measurement-less users, and is overwritten by the next measurement sync — measurements are the source of truth when present). Replaces the P2.2 ruling "PATCH with changed weightKg appends body_measurements".
- 2026-07-12 · Part 2B §3.2 S4 (P2.6a completion) · POST /v1/nutrition/meals accepts a UNION: photo confirm {scanToken,...} OR manual entry {mealName,...} (GAP-2 ruling honored — the rate-limited chat had shipped photo-confirm only); manual items resolve via food search, origin='manual', grams user-chosen so ranges collapse to the point, portion_source='default'.
- 2026-07-12 · 2B §3.3 (P2.6a T3 finding 1) · kcalPoint is CLAMPED into its own rounded [kcalLow, kcalHigh] — point used Math.round while bounds used round-to-10, so a collapsed range (user-chosen grams) could strand the point outside its range (165 vs [170,170]). Regression test pins the rounding rules.
- 2026-07-12 · R7.1 (P2.6a T3 finding 2, ruling) · Gamification's stats aggregator MAY read other modules' tables directly (workout/meal aggregates in gamification/repo.ts) — it is a cross-domain READ-ONLY aggregator by nature; requiring N service round-trips per badge evaluation would be ceremony without safety. Writes remain forbidden; the tables' owning repos stay the only writers.
- 2026-07-12 · 2B §3.2 S5 (P2.6a T3 finding 3) · PATCH grams edits PRESERVE each item's existing portion rung (matching confirmMeal); only items new to the meal get 'default'. Correction rows are stamped with the ORIGINAL items' worst rung — the rung that produced the corrected-away number is the Stage-5 telemetry signal.
- 2026-07-12 · (P2.6a T3 finding 4) · Dishware/measurement/delete handlers now call real service functions (one-liners) instead of repo re-exports — the module boundary keeps a seam for future authz/entitlement hooks.
- 2026-07-12 · (P2.6a T3, style) · The module's compressed one-liner code was REFORMATTED to house style before merge — repo.ts carries the tenancy WHEREs and must be reviewable at a glance (Part VI step 4).
- 2026-07-12 · Part 4 §3.6 (P2.6a T3 minor) · measuredAt gets the same ≤24h-future bound as takenAt — a far-future measurement would pin users.weight_kg as "latest" forever.
- 2026-07-12 · v1 §9.3 (P2.6a T3 residuals, ruled) · (a) Vision network/HTTP failures write NO ledger row — no completion was returned and no tokens billed; malformed-but-completed replies DO ledger their reported usage. (b) getLiveGymId + insertCostEvent run as two statements outside a tx — same accepted residual class as the coach ruling (outbox owed with the billing/worker phase). (c) A transient 500 after retake-token consumption eats the retake — accepted; the token is not payment-adjacent.
- 2026-07-12 · Sentry (P2.6a T3 verification) · P0.4's Sentry.init registers NO request-data integration; sendDefaultPii pinned false explicitly — 5xx captures attach only requestId, never bodies, so imageBase64 cannot leak via Sentry (2B §3.4 guarantee holds end-to-end).

## P2.6b — Geo/running READ-plan side rulings (2026-07-13)

- running.py:123 (P2.6b CORRECTION 1) · targetKm bound ported verbatim: `gt=0 / le=42.2 / default 5.0`. NO 0.5 floor exists in the salvage; none was added (a port copies constants, it does not invent sanity bounds — Part 0 rule 4).
- running.py:126,239 / v1 §9.3 (P2.6b CORRECTION 2 / G3) · Route generation ports count 1–5 default 3 and the seed schedule `11 + i*37`. Metering: ONE requireQuota("route_gen") slot per REQUEST; ONE api_cost_events row per ORS CALL ("every external API call writes a row"). A count=3 request therefore decrements quota once and writes three cost rows. A network/parse failure returns no completion → no row for that call (the P2.6a vision-ledger ruling, applied to ORS).
- v1 §12 (P2.6b G3) · Server-side route SCORING (score_and_rank) and WEATHER (check_weather) are NOT ported here — scoring is engine/mobile (§12 puts scoring in @app/engine), weather is a deferred follow-up. /v1/geo/routes/generate returns normalized ORS candidates only (coords, distanceM, elevationGainM, safe/busy/green fractions, isLoop).
- v1 §6.1 / §7.2 / §12 (P2.6b G1) · The two-layer geocode cache (Redis `geo:{lat3}:{lng3}` over the geo_cache Postgres floor, coords rounded to 3 decimals) ships now as MANDATED infra with an INJECTED resolver. It is intentionally RESOLVER-LESS in P2.6b — not dead code: the live reverse-geocode provider and its public endpoint defer to P5 because v1 §12 puts route-name geocoding-on-save on mobile. The seam's caching behavior is locked by the two-layer-cache integration test using a fake resolver.
- routing_provider.py:96-137,270-288 (P2.6b G2) · The salvage's MOCK route generator is a DELIBERATE salvage OMISSION — fabricated geodata violates the trust doctrine, and route_gen is fail-closed. No-key → typed 503; any ORS runtime failure → typed 503 operational (warn-logged, no Sentry — CoachError precedent). There is no offline/mock fallback.
- Part 4 §3.9 (P2.6b G4) · run_schedules is DEFERRED entirely: its `rule jsonb` shape is unspecified in Part 4 and nothing consumes schedules until notifications/mobile. `runs` is READ-ONLY here (list + detail); the run record/sync WRITE path is P5 (mobile, client-generated id + idempotency like workouts).
- v1 §9.3 / Part 0 rule 4 (P2.6b) · ORS cost constant `ORS_ROUTE_COST_MICRO = 0n` (apps/api/src/modules/geo/cost.ts): OpenRouteService's free/OSM tier has no per-call price, so the api_cost_events row is the §9.3 CALL-COUNT requirement (units=1, unit_type='route', provider='ors'), not a dollar charge. One-line updatable the day a paid ORS plan is adopted.
- Part 4 §3.9 (P2.6b minor) · `runs.splits` (jsonb) is OMITTED from the P2.6b read response — its shape is unspecified and P5 owns the writer; adding it now would invent a shape (R0.2). Run detail returns the polyline (owner-only) + core fields; splits join when P5 defines them.

## P2.7 — Mongo→PG migration rulings (2026-07-13; approved at the P2.7 gate)

- Part I §7b / Part 0 (P2.7 split) · P2.7 is split (like P2.6/P1.8) — one migration-worth per chat exceeds the 🔴 ceiling. Order: P2.7a INVENTORY (read-only, apps/api/tools/migrate-mongo/INVENTORY.md) → P2.7b harness + `users` → P2.7c workouts+sets+templates → P2.7d meal_logs+body_measurements → P2.7e coach+running → P2.7f verify gates + prod runbook.
- v1 §7.4 vs Part 4 §7 (P2.7 G3, spec-vs-spec CONFLICT) · v1 §7.4 says "ObjectId→UUID with a lookup table"; Part 4 §7:867-871 says UUIDv5-deterministic "without a lookup table". Part 4 §7 WINS (Part 0 precedence: later + more-specific part; Part 4 §0 amends earlier parts). Migration uses `id = UUIDv5(NAMESPACE_AIHG, mongo _id hex)`, NO lookup table; source id kept in `legacy_mongo_id`. CLAUDE.md:305/360 ("mapping table") echo the superseded v1 framing.
- v1 §7.4 argon2id vs Part 4 §7 bcrypt (P2.7, RESOLVED) · v1 §7.4's "upgrade to argon2id on next login" is moot — P2.1 shipped bcrypt-only (auth/service.ts:17 BCRYPT_COST=10; auth/repo.ts:70,86 write hash_algo='bcrypt'; DECISIONS 2026-07-11 GAP-1). Migration preserves Mongo bcrypt as hash_algo='bcrypt'; users.hash_algo CHECK allows argon2id but nothing writes it. No open conflict.
- Part 4 §7:868 (P2.7 G2) · NAMESPACE_AIHG frozen once = `4fc832e8-4827-4475-bf8a-e72cc4b611c7` (generated via crypto.randomUUID). Must NEVER change or every migrated id drifts. Referenced only at part4:868; no spec value existed, so this is the project-frozen value.
- R1.4 (P2.7 G4) · UUIDv5 = a ~30-line in-house SHA-1 RFC-4122 impl (tested against RFC vectors), NOT the `uuid` npm package — keeps the zero-extra-dep ethos; `node:crypto` provides only v4. Lands in P2.7b.
- P2.7a inventory OUTPUT · ~15 blocking items surfaced for P2.7b: the workout_sets unroll is DATA-ONLY (legacy `exercises` is an untyped list → needs a mongodump, G1); plus rulings owed on kcal recompute, unmatched-exercise fallback, embedded-subdoc id derivation, onboarding-field data loss, OAuth/role/profilePicture dispositions, meal_type/fiber drop, workouts-legacy-id column, running polyline encoding, and run_schedules.rule shape. See INVENTORY.md "BLOCKING items".

## P2.7 — clean-start + full-migration decisions (2026-07-13; Kd ruled)

- v1 §7.4 / Part 4 §7 / R0.1 (P2.7 migration approach = B) · The full P2.7 Mongo→PG migration IS built per §7 (spec is law; a DEVIATION PROPOSAL to skip it — on the grounds the old data is 18 dev/test users with no real user base — was RAISED and REJECTED by Kd; Part I.5 "rejected → spec stands"). The script is built AND verified end-to-end against the live dev Mongo (docker `aihg-mongo`) as the fixture: §7 gates (counts ±0, sampled user/workout/meal counts, 20 bcrypt logins, UUIDv5 cross-ref spot-checks).
- (P2.7 clean production start) · PRODUCTION launches with an EMPTY database — the dev/test data (18 users, 230 plan-level workouts, 16 meals, 11 coach threads, 8 runs, 17 routes, 1 schedule, 1 measurement) is NOT imported into production. Rationale: production-grade hygiene — importing test accounts/skeleton workouts would permanently contaminate real users, metrics, and Form Score™/leaderboards. The verified migration (above) is a correctness artifact + future insurance, not the production seed.
- (P2.7a inventory now DATA-VERIFIED, live Mongo scan 2026-07-13) · Corrections to the code-derived inventory: (1) auth+ml+onboarding are ONE merged `users` collection (18 docs; 17 password, 1 googleId, 18 email) — §7's "merge duplicate auth/ml email pairs" does NOT apply. (2) `workout_sessions.exercises[]` items are PRESCRIPTIONS `{id,name,category?,sets,reps,rest}` (or the full denormalized exercise doc) — 0 of 230 carry any per-set performance; the new `workout_sets` rich fields (rep_scores, fault_counts, form-per-set, view, mode, hold_ms, tempo, rom, calibration) have NO legacy source → all NULL; unroll = expand each item's `sets` count into set_index rows with prescribed `reps`. (3) `active_seconds_by_exercise` is NOT persisted → the §7 per-exercise MET kcal recompute is impossible; keep stored `calories_burned` (calc_version=0) [G-kcal, pending final ruling]. (4) `workout_templates` = 0 rows. (5) exercises = 58 (matches Part 0 canonical count).

## P2.7b — harness + users stage (2026-07-13; built approach B)
- R1.4 (approved A) · Added `mongodb` as an apps/api devDependency for the read-only migration reader; tsconfig include + lint now cover `apps/api/tools/`. UUIDv5 is the in-house RFC-4122 impl (uuid5.ts), no `uuid` package.
- weight.ts · Legacy `weight {value,unit}` → kg ports calories.py's KG_PER_LB (0.453592) but NOT its 70 kg estimation fallback: `users.weight_kg` is nullable, so missing/invalid weight migrates as NULL rather than a fabricated 70 (don't invent measurements).
- verify.ts · §7 bcrypt gate = migrated hash byte-equal to source AND valid bcrypt format ($2[aby]$). The literal "20 logins verify" needs plaintext creds we don't hold; equality+format is the automatable proxy. PROVEN: 17/17 password users intact.
- users.ts · The users stage writes ONLY `users` rows. Gamification (streaks/user_achievements) is NOT written here — §7:882-884 recomputes it from migrated workout history, so it lands in a later stage (after workouts). Onboarding fields dropped (no columns); OAuth googleId dropped (no table).
- PROVE (live dev Mongo → Neon branch) · read=18 transformed=18 skipped=0 inserted=18; re-run inserted=0 (idempotent); VERIFY count mongo=18/pg=18 ok; bcrypt sampled=17 intact=17 ok. Full suite 194/194 green.

## P2.7b — T3 fixes (2026-07-13, fresh-chat review, ALL resolved)
- (T3-1, gate integrity) mongo.count uses countDocuments (exact), not estimatedDocumentCount (metadata estimate) — §7 "counts match ±0" must not ride an estimate.
- (T3-2) insertUser ON CONFLICT targeted on (id) (idempotent re-run); a genuine unique collision (e.g. duplicate email) is now caught PER-ROW in run.ts — logged with its mongo _id + counted in `errors`, never silently dropped, never aborting the run (exit 1 if errors>0).
- (T3-3) unparseable legacy lastLogin → null lastActiveAt (Invalid Date would abort the insert).
- (T3-4) email parsed leniently (z.string(), not .email()) — a cosmetically-malformed email no longer discards the whole user + its bcrypt credentials; stored as-is or nulled.
- (T3-5) bcrypt verify gate is vacuously-ok on a 0-password (all-OAuth) set.
- (T3-6) weightToKg clamps >999.99 kg → null (numeric(5,2) overflow would abort the insert).
- Re-PROVE (fixed code, live Mongo → Neon, cleared first): dry-run 18/0; --apply inserted=18 errors=0, VERIFY count 18=18 (countDocuments) ok, bcrypt 17/17 ok; re-apply inserted=0 (idempotent). +3 robustness tests. Full suite 197/197 green.

## P2.7c — workouts + sets + gamification recompute (2026-07-13; Kd ruled at plan gate)
- (P2.7c GAP1 = a) exercise_id resolution uses an explicit **14-name→slug map** (tools/migrate-mongo/exerciseNames.ts, `NAME_TO_SLUG`) — the 230 legacy workouts reference EXACTLY 14 distinct exercise names (live-scanned). A name whose slug has no seeded catalog row TODAY (only squat/jump_squat/chair_squat are seeded; the other 55 land at P4) is SKIPPED + quality-flagged `unknown_exercise` (mirrors the sync path, workouts/repo.ts:70). The map is a REVIEWED CONSTANTS TABLE (P1.8a precedent): a purely mechanical derivation is WRONG — seeded slugs are singular ("squat", seed.ts:198) vs legacy plurals ("Squats") — so each row is hand-set with catalog evidence. 3 of 14 resolve today; 8 target canonical Part 2 §6 catalog slugs (reconcile at P4); 3 (Arnold Shoulder Press §6:731, 1-Arm Lat Pulldown, 1 Leg Box Squat) are NOT in the canonical 58 → permanently skipped.
- (P2.7c GAP1 re-run correction, T3-B finding 2026-07-13, Kd ruled) An INCREMENTAL re-run after P4 seeds a previously-skipped name does NOT self-heal (the earlier "resolves automatically on re-run" claim was WRONG): set_index is GLOBAL and assigned over currently-resolvable items only, so newly-resolved items shift indices and collide with already-persisted (workout_id, set_index) rows (ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING keeps the stale row); and the parent `workouts` row is ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING, so `sets_count`/`total_reps`/`quality_flags` never heal. To pick up exercises seeded after the initial migration, do a **CLEAN re-migrate** — clear the workout tables and re-run (from empty the indexing is consistent) — NOT an incremental re-run. Fine because production starts empty (this is a dev/test correctness artifact). Same-seed re-runs ARE a true no-op (deterministic UUIDv5 PK + ON CONFLICT), which is the idempotency property that matters during the migration window, and it is tested.
- (P2.7c GAP2) `workout_sets.duration_ms` is NOT NULL with no legacy source (0/230 carry per-set performance) → migrated as **0**.
- (P2.7c G-kcal, DEVIATION from §7 — T3-A finding, Kd ruled 2026-07-13) Spec §7 (04-part4-database.md:887-892) says legacy `kcal_*` is RECOMPUTED at migration with `calc_version=1` + the new MET table (2B). This is IMPOSSIBLE for the legacy data: `active_seconds_by_exercise` (per-exercise time, the MET input) was never persisted, and per GAP2 each migrated `workout_sets.duration_ms=0` — so a faithful recompute would zero out every workout's calories, destroying the real stored numbers. RULING: **keep the stored `calories_burned` at `kcal_calc_version=0`** (legacy provenance), NOT a §7 recompute. This is an explicit DEVIATION from §7's "one sanctioned history recomputation" clause, ratifying the P2.7a "[G-kcal, pending final ruling]" note (DECISIONS.md:205); the spec's recompute presumes an input the legacy schema never captured.
- (P2.7c GAP3) `workouts` keeps **UUIDv5-only traceability** (id = UUIDv5(sessionHex)); NO `legacy_mongo_id` column added, NO schema change / no migration. Set ids = UUIDv5(sessionHex:globalSetIndex) (G-subid).
- (P2.7c verify gate, Kd correction) The workouts count gate is honest w.r.t. fail-soft skipping: PG matched (by UUIDv5 id) must equal the number of MIGRATABLE sessions (transform-accepted = Mongo − skipped), and insert errors surface as PG < expected. Sets gate = PG rows on migrated workouts == Σ prescribed sets over RESOLVABLE names. Streaks gate = every migrated user with ≥1 workout has a streaks row. Gates re-derive expected via the tested pure transform (verifies the DB write path, not just the transform).
- (P2.7c recompute reuse) Gamification is recomputed by calling gamification/service.ts `onWorkoutSynced({sql}, userId, null)` per migrated user (NOT reimplemented) — it replays streaks from workout-day history and awards achievements idempotently (§7:882-884); `safeTimeZone(null)='UTC'` matches legacy utcnow day-math. Precondition: target DB seeded (exercises + achievements FK targets) — `tsx src/db/seed.ts` (idempotent).
- (P2.7c reader infra) mongo.ts reader generalized to normalize ALL top-level ObjectId fields → hex (was `_id` only), so `workout_sessions.user_id` arrives as a hex string and transforms stay string-typed/Zod-parseable (the reader's documented intent). Users stage unaffected (its only ObjectId is `_id`).

## P2.7d — meals + body_measurements (2026-07-13; Kd ruled at plan gate)
- (P2.7d live scan) meal_logs=16 (ONE user 6a0d468a…, a P2.7c workout user; keys meal_type/food_name/quantity/kcal/protein_g/carbs_g/fat_g/fiber_g/notes/consumed_at). body_measurements=1 (same user; weight_kg + 7 *_cm + body_fat_pct). Both target tables HAVE a `legacy_mongo_id` column (trust.ts) — traceability + verify gate use it (unlike workouts).
- (P2.7d GAP-A, the meal item — Kd ruled) `meal_logs.items` is NOT NULL jsonb re-validated by `@app/shared` mealItemSchema on EVERY read (nutrition/repo.ts:60 → a nonconforming item 500s the read). The schema REQUIRES per item: `gramsPoint>0`, `gramsRange:[>0,>0]`, `nutritionSource ∈ {curated,openfoodfacts}` (nutrition.ts:4-15) — legacy meals have NONE (no grams; `quantity` is a serving count, not grams; no source). RULING: build ONE synthetic item per meal — `gramsPoint=100`, `gramsRange=[70,130]` (placeholder), `portionSource='legacy'` (the honest "not a real measurement" flag; 'legacy' IS in the enum), `nutritionSource='curated'` (least-wrong; the enum has no legacy value). The ROW's `nutrition_sources=['legacy_model']` (text[], no enum) carries honest provenance. `quantity` is NOT used as grams (misleading). This fabricates a portion size, flagged via portionSource='legacy'; acceptable because production starts empty (dev/test correctness artifact).
- (P2.7d GAP-B, drop) `meal_type`, `fiber_g`, `notes`, `quantity` have no target column → dropped.
- (P2.7d GAP-C, origin) Some `notes` read "Estimated from photo…" but there is NO structured photo field → `origin='manual'` for all (conservative; the `photo_meal` badge simply won't award — never a fabricated 'photo').
- (P2.7d meal row fixed values) `confirmed=true` (user saved them), `kcal_low=round(kcal*0.7)`/`kcal_high=round(kcal*1.3)` (§7 ±30% band), `portion_source='legacy'`, `calc_version=0`, `legacy_mongo_id=_id`, ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING. Row totals = the single item (kcal/macros).
- (P2.7d GAP-D, weight refresh) After body inserts, `users.weight_kg` is refreshed to each user's LATEST measurement (mirrors nutrition/repo.ts refreshWeight; body history is the authoritative weight per §3.6/DECISIONS 2026-07-12) — overwrites the P2.7b-migrated weight for that user. weight_kg clamped >999.99→null (numeric(5,2)); `*_cm`+body_fat_pct → `metrics` jsonb (nonneg finite only, per the read-side z.record guard).
- (P2.7d GAP-E, gamification) Re-run gamification/service.ts `onMealLogged({sql}, userId, null)` per meal-affected user (REUSE, not reimpl) — awards meal badges (first_meal) idempotently; also lazily reconciles that user's streak to today (identical to their next live read, DECISIONS GAP-5; harmless for old data).

## P2.7d — T3 advisories (2026-07-13, fresh-chat review; CLEAN, no violations)
- (T3 advisory 1 — CONSCIOUS ACCEPT, out of scope) A later PATCH of a MIGRATED meal erodes its provenance: nutrition/repo.ts updateMeal recomputes portion_source via worst() and nutrition_sources via sources(), neither of which recognizes portionSource='legacy' / nutritionSource='legacy_model' — so an edit silently rewrites them to 'user_dishware' / ['curated'], losing the honest legacy flag. Pre-existing P2.6a code, NOT touched by P2.7d (R1 scope). Accepted because production starts EMPTY (no migrated meals exist to edit in prod); the migration is a dev/test artifact. Recorded so it is a deliberate accept, not an oversight.
- (T3 advisory 2 — deliberate, not a regression) verifyNutrition is count-only (transform-accepted == rows WHERE legacy_mongo_id IS NOT NULL), lighter than §7:911-914's per-table checksums + sampled per-user meal-count parity. Consistent with the count-gate doctrine already ruled for P2.7b/c; a recorded deliberate choice.

## P2.7e — coach + running (2026-07-13; Kd ruled at plan gate)
- (P2.7e live scan) coach_conversations=11 (32 messages total; roles only user/assistant, both ∈ CHECK; 0 empty content; all titles present; 2 distinct users). running_sessions=8 (duration_min missing in 2, calories_burned missing in 2, splits missing in 2 — all guarded). running_routes=17 (0 missing coords/label). running_schedules=1 (status='cancelled'). All coach/run/route user_ids are among the 18 migrated users (FK-safe). BSON types verified: no strings-as-numbers.
- (P2.7e GAP-F, G-poly, Kd ruled) runs.polyline / saved_routes.polyline are `text` and the app treats them as OPAQUE strings (shared/geo.ts:38 comments "polyline is opaque text passthrough (the §7 migration contract)", z.string()). Legacy path/coords ([lat,lng] arrays) → `JSON.stringify(array)`. No polyline encoder invented; reads won't break.
- (P2.7e GAP-G, G-rule) running_schedules DEFERRED (not migrated) — rule jsonb shape still unspecified (P2.6b/P2.7b deferral), n=1, and this one is 'cancelled'. run.ts logs it as deferred.
- (P2.7e GAP-H, NOT NULL defaults) runs.duration_s=round(duration_min*60) default 0 if missing; runs.distance_m=round(distance_km*1000); runs.kcal_point=round(calories_burned) or null; runs.kcal_calc_version=0 (keep-stored, workouts G-kcal precedent); runs.source='mobile'; runs.splits=jsonb or null; saved_routes.name=label or 'Legacy route'; saved_routes.distance_m=round(distance_km*1000); a route with empty coords is SKIPPED (polyline NOT NULL) — 0 such in the data. runs.started_at missing → skip (0 such).
- (P2.7e GAP-I, message ORDERING bug fix) coach_messages read path orders by (created_at DESC, id DESC) (coach/repo.ts:157), but id=UUIDv5(convHex:index) is a HASH, not index-ordered — and ALL 11 conversations have tied per-message timestamps (verified, maxDup 3), so raw timestamps cannot order messages. FIX: coach_messages.created_at = anchor + arrayIndex ms, where anchor = first message timestamp (else updated_at, else epoch). This makes created_at strictly monotonic in message order, so the read returns the original sequence. G-subid unchanged (id = UUIDv5(convHex:index)).
- (P2.7e no gamification step) coach/running do NOT feed the badge evaluator (gamification getStats has no coach_messages/runs keys) → no recompute; the first_chat badge won't auto-award (pre-existing, out of scope).

## P2.7e — T3 advisories (2026-07-13, fresh-chat review; CLEAN, no violations)
- (T3 advisory 1 — CONSCIOUS ACCEPT, spec-ruled) Migrated saved_routes store polyline as a JSON coord-array string (GAP-F passthrough) while natively-saved routes store a client-encoded polyline (geo.ts:39). A future client map-decoder must handle BOTH formats or migrated routes render wrong. Spec-sanctioned "polyline passthrough" (§7:901) + Kd-ruled GAP-F; production starts empty (no migrated routes in prod). Recorded as a deliberate accept.
- (T3 advisory 2 — FIXED) The coach ordering test now reads through the REAL production path getRecentMessages (outer sort created_at ASC, no id tiebreaker) instead of a hand-rolled SELECT, so it guards the actual read path against a GAP-I regression.
- (T3 advisory 3 — pre-existing, out of scope) The live coach read path (coach/repo.ts getRecentMessages) has no id tiebreaker on equal created_at, so live same-millisecond user/assistant pairs could invert. Predates P2.7e; the migration deliberately sidesteps it (created_at monotonic per thread). Not fixed here — flagged for a future coach-module task.

## P2.7f — verify gates + prod runbook (2026-07-13; Kd-approved scope)
- (P2.7f scope, Kd-confirmed) P2.7f = strengthened §7 verify gates + a production cutover RUNBOOK document — NOT the live cutover. DECISIONS:194 defines the split as "verify gates + prod runbook"; the actual cutover is P2.8 and is premature: apps/web still calls the OLD services for auth/coach/ml/nutrition (only syncClient → new API), argon2id rehash is owed before P2.8, and Phases 3–6 are unbuilt (backend-auth/backend-ml still the salvage source). So P2.7f repoints/freezes/decommissions NOTHING.
- (P2.7f deep gate) verify.ts adds comparePerUser (pure) + verifyParity: PER-USER count parity across workouts/sets/meals/coach_messages/runs (catches a mis-attributed row that keeps the aggregate right) + a UUIDv5 cross-ref spot-check (§7 "workout→user": sampled migrated workout's PG user_id == uuidv5(source user_id)). PG per-user counts scoped to migrated users (workouts/sets have no legacy_mongo_id). Wired into run.ts verify.
- (P2.7f --verify-only) run.ts gains a --verify-only mode (forces apply=false → runs the gates with NO writes; the runbook's re-verify step) and logs elapsed time (§7 single-digit-minute target). PROVE: --verify-only on the rebuilt Neon → all gates ok, per_user_mismatches=0, crossref_ok=true, 12.5s.
- (P2.7f runbook refinement) RUNBOOK/cutover.md: the §7 step-3/4 procedure to execute at P2.8, with the prerequisite checklist. Refinement caught while writing it — "flip DATA_BACKEND=pg" (spec prose) is really the seeded `data_backend` feature_flags row (seed.ts:205), NOT an env var; the runbook says so.

## P2.7f — T3 review (2026-07-13, fresh chat)
- (T3 finding A — FIXED) The UUIDv5 cross-ref spot-check sampled ALL workout_sessions with string _id/user_id, ignoring migratability — so a transform-skipped (or insert-errored) session, legitimately absent from PG, flipped crossRefOk=false: a fail-closed FALSE failure that could spuriously red the §7 "gates green on a real dump" Done gate. Now samples only transform-accepted workouts (transformWorkout(doc)!==null), using row.id/row.userId — absence-from-PG can no longer masquerade as a mapping failure. (PROVE passed pre-fix only because the dev set's first 20 were all migratable.)
- (T3 advisory — asymmetric per-user scoping, ACCEPTED) verifyParity scopes meals/coach/runs by legacy_mongo_id (exact) but workouts/workout_sets by "migrated user" because those tables have NO legacy_mongo_id column. Consequence: a migrated user who ALSO has a natively-synced workout would inflate the PG per-user count → a false mismatch on a MIXED db. Harmless on greenfield prod (empty) and on the verified dev cutover (per_user_mismatches=0); best available given the schema. Recorded so a future re-run against a mixed db isn't surprised. No fix.
- (T3 other) SQL parameterized (ANY(${ids}), values only); Date.now is tool-timing, not engine (R5.1 scoped to packages/engine); comparePerUser pure + unit-tested; runbook correctly defers the live cutover to P2.8. CLEAN otherwise.

## argon2id rehash-on-login (2026-07-14; the P2.1 GAP-1 deferral, owed before P2.8)
- (v1 §6.1 / R1.4) Password hashing upgraded to argon2id via `@node-rs/argon2` (prebuilt native binaries, no node-gyp; ships its own .d.ts). `bcryptjs` KEPT — it verifies legacy bcrypt hashes. Params = OWASP minimum: memoryCost 19456 KiB, timeCost 2, parallelism 1 (`ARGON2ID_OPTS`, service.ts). `algorithm` passed as the numeric literal `2` (Algorithm.Argon2id) with `as const`: the library exports Algorithm as a `const enum`, which cannot be imported as a value under isolatedModules (R2.1/TS2748); the `$argon2id$` + `m=19456,t=2,p=1` output format is asserted in the unit tests as the guard.
- (v1 §6.1) `PasswordHasher` interface changed from `{ hash, compare }` to `{ algo, hash, verify(pw,hash,algo), needsRehash(algo) }`. `verify` dispatches on the STORED algo (bcrypt→bcrypt.compare, argon2id→argon2.verify) with an exhaustive `never` default (R2.4). NB argon2.verify(hashed, password) takes the hash FIRST (opposite to bcrypt.compare) — pinned by the round-trip unit test. Only auth service.ts (defines) + routes.ts (wires) consume it; no other module.
- (v1 §6.1) Rehash-on-login: after a successful verify, `if (needsRehash(user.hashAlgo)) setPasswordHash(argon2id hash)`. BEST-EFFORT — a rehash write failure is swallowed + logged (event `auth.password_rehash_failed`) and NEVER fails an already-valid login (deliberate R2.5 exception, same swallow pattern as the existing email sends). Awaited (not fire-and-forget) so the upgrade is durable before the session returns AND observable by the routes test. Migrated bcrypt users upgrade transparently on their next login; a re-login is a byte-identical no-op (needsRehash false).
- (v1 §6.1 accepted limitation) The timing-equalizer DUMMY_HASH is now a single module-level argon2id hash. One dummy cannot equalize login timing across a MIXED bcrypt+argon2id population: a not-yet-upgraded bcrypt user's login (bcrypt.compare) times differently from an unknown-email attempt (argon2.verify on the dummy) until that user rehashes. Self-healing as the population migrates; accepted rather than maintaining two dummies.
- (defense-in-depth) A non-null password_hash always carries a valid hash_algo (migration invariant, tools/migrate-mongo/collections/users.ts:65: password null ⇒ algo null, else 'bcrypt'), and login short-circuits on password_hash null before any verify — so hash_algo-null-with-a-hash is unreachable. Still handled: login/changePassword treat a null hashAlgo as fail-closed (invalid_credentials / no_password) rather than crashing. `repo.toHashAlgo` validates the nullable text column into `HashAlgo | null`.
- (no schema migration) users.hash_algo CHECK already allows 'argon2id' (identity.ts:37, migration 0001) — this card adds NO migration. `DUMMY_HASH` exported from service.ts solely so a unit test can assert it is argon2id.

## web repoint — Card 1: auth → httpOnly-cookie on the new /v1 API (2026-07-15)
- (SCOPE, Kd-ruled) The "apps/web repointed to the new API" P2.8 prerequisite is NOT a config flip: the web ran the OLD auth model (localStorage access/refresh tokens + `Authorization: Bearer` on every client) while the new API is httpOnly-cookie-only with no tokens in any response body (shared/src/auth.ts:52 authUserSchema; DECISIONS 2026-07-11). v1 §13 lists the SPA's changes but does not itemize this repoint; v1 §6.1:439 fixes the target model ("rotating refresh tokens (httpOnly cookie on web)"). Decomposed into cards: ①auth ②users/profile ③progress/gamification/exercises ④coach ⑤nutrition ⑥running/geo ⑦recommendation-or-drop. Card ① is this one.
- (BRANCH STRATEGY, Kd-ruled) The repoint cannot be staged client-by-client on a working app — auth is all-or-nothing (cookie vs localStorage), so the moment auth flips, every not-yet-repointed client loses the localStorage token it depends on. Therefore ALL web-repoint cards land as commits on ONE branch `web-repoint` (each with its own T3), and it merges to master only when the web fully runs on the new API — which IS the P2.8 cutover moment. `master` stays deployable (old-backend web) until then (Part I §6).
- (Google OAuth DEFERRED, Kd-ruled) v1 §6.1:438 lists Google OAuth in the auth module, but P2.1 shipped email/password only and the new API has NO /v1/auth/google route (verified). So the web's Google buttons are hidden behind `GOOGLE_LOGIN_ENABLED=false` (Login.jsx, Register.jsx) and the /auth/google/success route + import are removed from App.jsx. Google OAuth on the new API is its own later card; `pages/GoogleAuthSuccess.jsx` is left in-tree but is now ORPHANED dead code (no route, no import) that still does `localStorage.setItem('accessToken')` (:31) and calls the now-wrong `/auth/me` path (:35) — unreachable, so inert; the Google card must rewrite or delete it (T3 finding).
- (INTERIM behavior regression — onboarding gating goes inert) `authUserSchema` (shared/src/auth.ts:52) has NO `onboardingCompleted` field — onboarding is a `users`-module concern per v1 §6.1:442, not auth — so /v1/auth/me and /v1/auth/login never return it. The three sites that gate on it — ProtectedRoute.jsx:44, :54 and Login.jsx:33 — compare `=== false`, so `undefined` makes them all false: **onboarding is never enforced for anyone** on this branch. Fails OPEN (no redirect loop, no user trap), and the branch can't reach master before P2.8, so it is accepted as an interim — but it IS a real regression, and restoring it is OWED BY the users/profile card (②) when /v1/users/me supplies the field. (T3 finding: was undocumented; recorded here.)
- (INTERIM known-broken — the still-old clients) Login no longer writes `localStorage.accessToken`, so every not-yet-repointed client that injects `Bearer <localStorage token>` now sends `Bearer null` and breaks ON THE BRANCH: mlApi.js:10, coachApi.js:17, nutritionApi.js:31, utils/storage.js:28, and Settings.jsx:401 (handleDeleteAccount, untouched by Card 1). This is the planned multi-card consequence, not a defect — PROVE for Card 1 must NOT be expected to exercise coach / ML-profile / nutrition / delete-account. Each is repointed by its own card before the branch merges. (T3 finding: recorded explicitly.)
- (compat shim, interim) The new user shape exposes `displayName`; 6 display sites still read `user?.fullName` (+1 `user?.profilePicture`, which the new shape lacks and which stays undefined behind optional chaining). `normalizeUser` in AuthContext.jsx aliases `fullName: displayName` on both getMe and login so Card 1 stays contained to the auth layer. The users/profile card (②) migrates those sites and REMOVES the shim.
- (R10.2 reconciled) The authApi 401→refresh→retry-once interceptor retries a POST, which R10.2 forbids for network-failure retries. Reconciled: the retry fires ONLY on `error.response.status === 401` (network errors have no `.response` → never retried), and in this API auth is a preHandler — a 401 is rejected BEFORE the handler runs, so the write never executed and re-sending after cookie rotation is safe. Capped at one retry via `config._retried`; /v1/auth/login and /v1/auth/refresh are excluded from the interceptor so it cannot recurse. (T3 confirmed the reasoning holds.)

## web repoint — Card 2: per-user storage keying + displayName migration (2026-07-15)
- (CARD-1 REGRESSION — found in Card 2 grounding, FIXED here) `utils/storage.js getUserId()` decoded the JWT out of `localStorage.accessToken` to key every per-user bucket (`userKey(k) = user_${id}_${k}`). Card 1 moved sessions to httpOnly cookies (v1 §6.1), which JS cannot read — so getUserId() returned **'guest' for every account, permanently**. Consumers: ActiveWorkout, ExerciseLibrary, PreWorkout, WorkoutBuilder, and syncQueue.js:13 (the OFFLINE SYNC QUEUE). Impact: all accounts on a device shared one bucket, so on a shared browser user A's unflushed workouts sat in the same queue B would flush under B's cookie → workouts mis-attributed to the wrong user, against R10.3's intent. FIX: `storage.setCurrentUserId(id)` is pushed in by AuthContext on session-restore/login/logout; getUserId() returns it ('guest' when logged out). Regression tests: utils/storage.test.js (isolation, logout→guest, per-user queue buckets, and an explicit guard that keying no longer derives from a token). NB Card 1's T3 saw storage.js:28 but classified it as ordinary Bearer-null breakage — it was a keying break, not an API-call break.
- (PLAN AMENDMENT, declared at implementation — R1.2) `syncClient.js`'s module-scope app-load flush (`flushSyncQueue()` at import) was REMOVED. It only ever worked because the user id was synchronously decodable from a localStorage JWT; with the id now arriving asynchronously from /v1/auth/me, an import-time flush reads the 'guest' bucket and would silently never flush the signed-in user's queued workouts. AuthContext kicks that flush immediately after adopting the session id (`adoptSession`). The `online` listener STAYS at module scope.
- (NOT EQUIVALENT — accepted delta, T3 Card 2) The AuthContext-kicked flush is **not** a like-for-like replacement for the removed import-time flush, and the code comment previously implied it was. Old: fired on EVERY app load (the id was synchronously in localStorage). New: fires only when `/v1/auth/me` SUCCEEDS. So if getMe fails at app load (network blip / API 5xx) while the session cookie is still valid, the id stays 'guest', no flush runs for that page-session, and the `online` listener can then only address the empty guest bucket. ACCEPTED: it is a **liveness delay bounded by one page-session, never data loss** — the queue is untouched and any later reload or login flushes it — and it is intrinsic to the cookie migration (there is no token to read offline). Recorded rather than fixed.
- (Kd-ruled — LEGACY BUCKET ORPHANING; scope corrected by T3) Per-user localStorage buckets — including the offline workout queue — are keyed `user_<id>_*`. Pre-repoint that id is the OLD Mongo ObjectId (from the old JWT); post-repoint it is the new UUID (§7 uuidv5 mapping), so unflushed workouts + WorkoutBuilder drafts orphan under an address the app no longer reads. NOT fixed in code: the client cannot derive mongoId from uuid, so a shim would have to "adopt any orphaned non-guest bucket" — reintroducing the shared-browser hazard this card just closed. **SCOPE (T3 correction): this is a dev/test-cutover issue, NOT a production hazard** — production launches with an EMPTY database (DECISIONS 2026-07-13, recorded at RUNBOOK/cutover.md:11-13), so there are no users and no buckets to orphan. NOW A CHECKBOX in RUNBOOK/cutover.md's prerequisites (that file already existed — T3 rightly flagged that "owed by a future card" is how such an item gets lost).
- (T3 Card 2 finding 1 — FIXED, R10.3) `flushRun` bound the queue KEY once at run start, but identity is bound at SEND time: `postSync` carries whatever httpOnly cookie is ambient and has no user binding. A session change mid-run (logout + a different login while the loop awaits) would keep draining user A's bucket while the server attributed those workouts to user B — the residual half of the same shared-browser hazard this card set out to close. FIX: `flushRun` captures `const owner = getUserId()` and returns immediately before any `post(entry)` if identity has moved; the abandoned entries stay queued for their real owner (neither dropped nor sent). Regression test: syncQueue.test.js "abandons the run when the session changes mid-flush". NB `syncApi` (syncClient.js:21-25) has NO timeout, so a hung POST holds a run open indefinitely and widens the window — the owner check makes that harmless (correctness no longer depends on run length), so the timeout is REPORTED not fixed (R1.1), for the sync card.
- (LANDMINE recorded for the Google OAuth card — T3, report-only) AuthContext exposes `setUser` raw in the context value (:123) and `pages/GoogleAuthSuccess.jsx:37` calls it directly — that path would set a session WITHOUT `adoptSession`, keying the user to 'guest' and re-opening the bucket/queue hazard. Currently UNREACHABLE (Card 1 deleted the route + import; the file is orphaned), so not a live defect and not fixed here (R1.1). The Google OAuth card MUST route session adoption through `adoptSession` (or stop exporting `setUser`).
- (interim shim REMOVED as promised) The 6 `user?.fullName` sites (Sidebar.jsx:149,153,188; Coach.jsx:519; Dashboard.jsx:262; Running.jsx:136) now read `user?.displayName`, and Card 1's `normalizeUser` alias is deleted from AuthContext.
- (scope, verified) `userService.getProfile/updateProfile` are DEAD CODE — the only live userService call is Onboarding.jsx:244 `completeOnboarding`, which is blocked by the onboarding-storage gap. Settings' two profile forms are ~80% onboarding fields (age/gender/height/targetWeight; fitnessLevel/fitnessGoals/availableEquipment/sessionDuration/preferredWorkoutTime/exerciseFrequency/medicalConditions) and its avatar upload has no target — so Settings' profile surface, the Onboarding wizard and the 3 onboarding gate sites are all LEFT UNTOUCHED on the old backend. Nothing deleted; all owed to the onboarding-storage card.
- (out of scope, REPORTED not fixed — R1.1) `syncClient.test.js` "is a no-op when VITE_API_URL is not configured" FAILS ON MASTER TOO (verified by stashing: identical failure, 1 failed/6 passed). Vitest loads `apps/web/.env`, which sets VITE_API_URL, so flushSyncQueue()'s guard doesn't fire and flush() reaches `window.localStorage` in the node env. The test's comment assumes VITE_API_URL is unset — true in CI (no .env committed), false on a dev machine. Pre-existing env-dependent test bug; not touched by this card.
- (testing gap, honest) The `adoptSession → flushSyncQueue` coupling is NOT unit-tested — that needs jsdom/RTL, which the web package doesn't have (vitest.config.js is node-only; adding it = new deps, R1.4). Covered only by the manual browser smoke. The storage keying itself IS unit-tested.

## web repoint — Card 3: progress + measurements (2026-07-16)
- (PROCESS DEVIATION, Kd-instructed) This card ran in the SAME chat that delivered the onboarding-storage card, against Part I §1's one-card-one-chat rule — Kd explicitly instructed "continue in this chat" twice. Recorded, not hidden. The chat re-grounded on the web-repoint branch per S1 before planning.
- (SCOPE AMENDMENT, declared mid-card per the plan's own stop-and-report clause) The approved plan optimistically included "exercises list + gamification streak portions". Grounding disproved both: (a) ExerciseLibrary renders display names, instructions, and media GIFs with server-side search — the Part 4 §3.4 exercises table (verified: db/schema/catalog.ts) stores NONE of that ({slug,nameKey,family,tier,tracking,met,difficulty,equipment,muscles}, .strict() query {limit,cursor} only); (b) BOTH gamification consumers (GamificationStrip, Achievements) render XP/levels + the badge catalog by category + leaderboard — the new API's whole surface is /v1/gamification/me {streak, earned achievements}, per the 2026-07-11 P2.3 rulings (GAP-1 XP deferred; carve: badges-screens/challenges/leaderboards later). Per the NO-REMOVAL rule both stay FULLY on the old backend, documented in their client headers, each line owed in RUNBOOK/cutover.md's prerequisites. Card 3's implemented repoint = Progress page (6 chart reads) + MeasurementsTracker.
- (repoint mapping, for the record) /progress/{overview,calories-trend,weekly-workouts,activity-heatmap,category-distribution,personal-records} → /v1/progress/{overview,trend,weekly,heatmap,distribution,records} (P2.3 contracts, @app/shared progress.ts); measurements CRUD → /v1/nutrition/body-measurements (Part 4 §3.6 home, built P2.6). Client-side adaptations, all data-shaped per the contracts' own comments: overview snake→camel + totalDurationMs→hours at render; trend points {date,kcal}; weekly {isoYear,isoWeek}→"Wnn" label; heatmap ARRAY→date-keyed map (the calendar grid's lookup shape); distribution families/sets feed the pie; records OBJECT of refs→display list with client labels/emoji; measurements rows flattened ({...metrics, weight_kg: weightKg}) and `latest` derived client-side (old backend precomputed it; new contract is data-shaped). POST measurement = .strict() {measuredAt: now, weightKg?, metrics{}} assembled in the component.
- (OUT OF SCOPE, reported per R1.1) WorkoutCalendar (inside the Progress page) rides workoutApi.getHistory → OLD backend. /v1/workouts EXISTS, so this is a pure client repoint — but workoutApi was not in the approved plan (workouts domain, not this card's three). Left untouched; owed line added to cutover.md.
- (test-env note) progressApi.test.js stubs `localStorage` for the getPredictions case — mlApi's request interceptor reads it and the web tests run in node env (vitest.config.js). mlApi itself untouched (browser-only, dies at P2.8).
- (SEQUENCING FLAG for Kd, recorded not ruled) The playbook sequences P4 (leaderboards) AFTER P2.8, but cutover.md:41 requires every feature's endpoint to exist before old-backend decommission. At the cutover checkbox Kd must either land a minimal leaderboard read early or explicitly accept a dark window — the owed-lines block in cutover.md says exactly this at the decision point.
- (T3 RESOLUTION, Card 3, 2026-07-16) Four findings, all real. FIXED: (f.1) weightKg rounded to 2dp + positive/<1000 guard and metrics clamped finite/nonnegative client-side before POST — the free-typed number input could produce values the .strict() contract 400s on (nutrition.ts:98-99); (f.3) weekly chart label carries the ISO year ("W29 ’25") whenever the point set spans years — Wnn alone collides in 1y/all views; (f.4) `limitedToDays` ignored by the Progress UI is now an owed line in RUNBOOK/cutover.md (T3 judged it P2-Done-gate-relevant: shipping plan-clamped data labeled as the full window misrepresents the P2.4 history gate). ACCEPTED-AND-RECORDED, not fixed: (f.2) `latest` measurement tiles derive from the newest 60 rows only, vs the old backend's full-history precompute — a metric last logged >60 entries ago drops off the tiles until logged again. Accepted: 60 entries ≈ months of logging; a cursor-walk to fill every metric key costs N round-trips for a cosmetic tile; revisit only if users notice. Also recorded (T3 security pass): measuredAt is client-clock now() — a >24h-fast clock gets a hard 400, a skewed clock mis-dates rows the old server-timestamped flow dated correctly; acceptable interim, noted here.

- (CROSS-SCOPE BUG FIX, declared — found by the Card 4 browser smoke, 2026-07-16) @fastify/cors v11 defaults `methods` to 'GET,HEAD,POST', so EVERY browser DELETE/PATCH/PUT to the API died at CORS preflight and never left the browser: coach thread delete (the smoke's step 8), PATCH /v1/users/me, the DPDP DELETE /v1/users/me, body-measurements/meals/dishware CRUD. Invisible to the whole test suite because fastify.inject() bypasses CORS — this class of bug is only catchable by a real browser (or an explicit preflight assertion). FIX (apps/api/src/app.ts, one line): methods GET,HEAD,POST,PUT,PATCH,DELETE. R9.5 honored: failing preflight test written FIRST (app.test.ts, red on 'GET,HEAD,POST'), then the fix, then green. API-side change inside a web card = declared here, not silent; it is a Part IV #6-class CORS trap worth adding to a future trap list.
